муассисаҳои таҳсилоти умумӣ

бо тағйиру иловаҳо

тасдиқ кардааст

Хонандагони азиз!
Китоб манбаи донишу маърифат аст. Аз он баҳравар шавед ва 
онро тоза нигоҳ доред! Кӯшиш кунед, ки соли таҳсили оянда ҳам 
ин китоб ҳамин гуна тозаву озода дастраси хонандагони дигар гар-
дад ва онҳо низ аз он истифода баранд.
Ҷадвали истифодаи китоб

answer the questions.

ба саволҳо ҷавоб дода тавонанд.

LESSON 1. AT SCHOOL
Competency: Pupils should have the skills of reading, 
writing, and understanding the content of the words and 
answer the questions.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд маҳорати хондан, навиш-

Ба расм нигариста ба саволҳо ҷавоб диҳед.
THE DAY OF KNOWLEDGE
1. What is this?
2. Is it a big or a small school?
3. Whom do you see in the picture?

7
4. Is he happy to be at school again?
5. Is it the Day of knowledge?
II. Read the dialogue. Муколамаро хонед.
Nozanin: Good morning.
Komron: Good morning.
Nozanin: Do you like your summer holidays?
Komron: Yes, I do. I like it very much.
Nozanin: Where did you go in your summer holidays to?
Komron: I went to the village.
Nozanin: Are you happy to be at school again?
Komron: Yes, I am.
Nozanin: What form are you in?
Komron: I am in form 4.
Nozanin: All right, see you.
III. Work in pairs. Act the dialogue.
Ду нафарӣ шуда кор кунед.
Муколама тартиб диҳед.
— Do you like...?
— Yes, I do.
— No, I don’t.
IV . Play a game “Champion”.
Бозии “Champion”-po иҷро намоед.
V . Write the words. Калимаҳоро нависед.
The Day of knowledge, school, summer
holidays, to be at school again, to be happy.
Speak about the Day of knowledge.
Дар бораи рӯзи Дониш нақл кунед.7

LESSON 3. HUMAN BODY
Competence:  Pupils  should  have  the  skills  of  reading,
writing, and understanding the meaning of the words and
answer the questions.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд маҳорати хондан, навиш-
тан ва фаҳмидани маънои калимаҳоро дошта бошанд,
ба саволҳо ҷавоб дода тавонанд.
Words:   hand, nose, mouth, eye and ear
Structure: Is this your...?
    Yes, it is. This is my....
I. Look at the picture and make up sentences. Ба расм нигоҳ карда, ҷум-
лаҳо тартиб диҳед.
II. Read the words. Калимаҳоро хонед.
Aa [ei] — plate, take, date, Kate, table.
[æ] — bag, at, stand, back, rat, fat.
[a:] — class, glass, past, last.
[a:] — dark, mark, lark, far.
III. Look at the picture and complete the sentences. Ба расм нигоҳ карда, 
ҷумлаҳоро пурра кунед.
1. It has a____________mouth.
2. It has___________paws.
3. The dog’s eyes are_______.
4. It’s ears are_____.

LESSON 3. HUMAN BODY
Competence: Pupils should have the skills of reading, 
writing, and understanding the content of the words and 
answer the questions.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд маҳорати хондан, навиш-
тан ва фаҳмидани мазмуни калимаро дошта бошанд, 
ба саволҳо ҷавоб дода тавонанд.
Words:  hand, nose, mouth, eye and ear
Structure:  Is this your ...?
   Yes, it is. This is my....
I. Look at the picture and write sentences. Ба расм нига-
риста ҷумлаҳо нависед.
hand  nose  mouth    eye     ear
II. Read the words. Калимаҳоро хонед.
Aa [ei] — plate, take, date, Kate, table.
[æ] — bag, at, stand, back, rat, fat.
[a:] — class, glass, past, last.
[a:] — dark, mark, lark, far.
III. Look at the picture and complete the sentences. Ба 
расм нигариста ҷумлаҳоро пурра намоед.
1. It has a____________mouth.
2. It has___________paws.
3. The dog's eyes are_______.
4. It’s ears are_____.

IV . Answer the questions. Ба саволҳо ҷавоб диҳед.
1. Is your mouth small or big?
2. Are your eyes brown?
3. Your nose is big, isn’t it?
4. What do you see with?
V . Read these words and make up sentences with them. Калимаҳои зерин-
ро хонед ва ба онҳо ҷумлаҳо тартиб диҳед.
  mouth     long
  nose     dirty
My ears  is or are   small
  hands     black
  eyes     brown
Read these words and make up sentences.
Калимаҳои зеринро хонед ва бо онҳо ҷумлаҳо
тартиб диҳед.
Mouth, hand, eyes, ears.
LESSON 4. HUMAN BODY
Competence:  Pupils  should  have  the  skills  of  reading,
writing, and understanding the meaning of the words and
answer the questions.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд маҳорати хондан, навиш-
тан ва фаҳмидани маънои калимаҳоро дошта бошанд,
ба саволҳо ҷавоб дода тавонанд.
Words:  head, hair, neck, shoulder, and face.
Structure: Is this your face or your neck?
    This is my _______.11
IV. Answer the questions. Ба саволҳо ҷавоб диҳед.
1. Is your mouth small or big?
2. Are your eyes brown?
3. Your nose is big isn’t it?
4. What do you see with?

маҳои зерин ҷумлаҳо тартиб диҳед.
  mouth      long 
  nose       dirty
My ears  is or are    small
  hands      black
  eyes       brown
Read these words and make up sentences. 
Калимаҳои зеринро хонед ва бо онҳо ҷумлаҳо 
тартиб диҳед.
Mouth, hand, eyes, ears.

LESSON 1. AT SCHOOL
Competency: Pupils should have the skills of reading, 
writing, and understanding the content of the words and 
answer the questions.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд маҳорати хондан, навиш-
тан ва фаҳмидани мазмуни калимаро дошта бошанд, 
ба саволҳо ҷавоб дода тавонанд.
I. Look and answer the questions.
Ба расм нигариста ба саволҳо ҷавоб диҳед.
THE DAY OF KNOWLEDGE
1. What is this?
2. Is it a big or a small school?
3. Whom do you see in the picture?

12
I. Look at the picture and make up sentences with them. Ба расм нигоҳ 
карда, ҷумлаҳои худатонро тартиб диҳед.
II. Read the words. Калимаҳоро хонед.
[e] red, pen, head
[ɛə] air, chair, hair
[∫] she, shelf, shoulder
[ei] cake, name, face
You have two eyes. You have two ears. You see with your eyes. You 
hear with your ears.
III. Look at the picture and answer the question. Ба расм нигоҳ карда, ба 
савол ҷавоб диҳед.
Is this her head or her face?
IV . Answer the questions. Ба саволҳо ҷавоб диҳед:
1. Is your head small?
2. Is your hair short or long?
3. Your face is round, isn’t it?
4. What do we hear with?
5. Is your neck short or long?
V . Make up sentences with these words. Бо ин ка-
лимаҳо ҷумла нависед.

This, my, is, head.    Is, his, big, shoulder.
Face, round, her, is.    The, girl, hair, has, brown.
My, is, long, neck.
Write these words and make up sentences.
Калимаҳоро нависед ва бо онҳо ҷумла тартиб диҳед.
Face, hair, head, neck, shouider.
Find a riddle. Чистонро ёбед.
Two brothers live across a pass,
But never see each other.
LESSON 5. HUMAN BODY
Competence:  Pupils  should  have  the  skills  of  reading,
writing, and understanding the meaning of the words and
answer the questions.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд маҳорати хондан, навиш-
тан ва фаҳмидани маънои калимаҳоро дошта бошанд,
ба саволҳо ҷавоб дода тавонанд.
Words:  arm, back, feet, toes, finger, teeth.
Structure: Is this your right (left) arm?
    This is my right (left) arm?
I. Look at the picture and make up sentences. Ба расм нигоҳ карда, ҷум-
лаҳо тартиб диҳед.

LESSON 5. HUMAN BODY
Competence: Pupils should have the skills of reading, 
writing, and understanding the content of the words and 
answer the questions.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд маҳорати хондан, навиш-
тан ва фаҳмидани мазмуни калимаро дошта бошанд, 
ба саволҳо давоб дода тавонанд.
Words:  arm, back, feet, toes, finger, teeth.
Structure:  Is this your right (left)...?
   This is my right (left)...?
I. Look at the picture and make up sentences. Ба расм 
нигариста ҷумлаҳо тартиб диҳед.
    arm          back           foot        toes     finger         teeth

14
II. Read the text. Матнро хонед.
Look! You have two hands. There are five fingers on each your 
hand. You have two feet. There are five toes on your right foot and five 
toes on your left foot.
III. Learn the poem. Шеърро аз ёд кунед.
Ten fingers   A baby says
Ten toes    When she smells the rose
Two eyes   “Oh, what a pity
And one nose   I’ve only one nose”!
to smell [smel] – бӯй кардан
pity [pıtı] – афсӯс
only [ounlı] – танҳо
IV . Look at the picture and show the parts of 
the body. Ба расм нигоҳ карда, узвҳои баданро 
нишон диҳед.
V . Fill in the blanks and write up sentences, (toes, fingers, long, teeth). Ҷой-
ҳои холиро пур кунед ва ҷумлаҳоро нависед.
My mother has _________ feet and arms.
Her ________ and __________ are long too.
She has a small __________.
Write these words and make up your own sentences.
Ин калимаҳоро нависед ва бо онҳо ҷумлаҳо тартиб
диҳед.
Arm, back, feet, finger, teeth, toes.

LESSON 6. HUMAN BODY
Competence:  Pupils  should  have  the  skills  of  reading,
writing, and understanding the meaning of the words and
answer the questions.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд маҳорати хондан, навиш-
тан ва фаҳмидани маънои калимаҳоро дошта бошанд,
ба саволҳо ҷавоб дода тавонанд.
Words:  pretty, sweet, pleasant, tasty, bald
Structure:  This is a... girl, isn’t she?
I. Read the poem. Шеърро хонед.
Little eyes see pretty things,
Little nose smells what is sweet.
Little ears hear pleasant sounds,
Mouth likes tasty things to eat.
things [Ɵıƞz] – чизҳо
to hear [hɛə] – шунидан
II. Read the words. Калимаҳоро хонед.
pencil, pen, pretty
tea, meet, sweet
ten, toy, tasty
ball, board, bald
III. Look at the picture and complete the sentences. Ба 
расм нигоҳ карда, ҷумлаҳоро пурра кунед.
1. This is my friend. His name _______ .
2. He has ______,________,________.

1. We have got ________ eyes.
 a) 2;  b) 1;  c) 3.
2. We see with our _______.
 a) nose; b) eyes; c) ears.
3. We can smell and breathe with our ______.
 a) shoulder; b) neck; c) nose.
4. We can eat and speak with our_______.
 a) mouth; b) head; c) trunk.
5. We walk with our_________.
 a) hand; b) teeth; c) feet.
2. Choose the correct translation. Ҷавоби дурустро интихоб кунед.
  a) head   a) eye   a) leg
 мӯй b) hair  гӯш b) trunk     даҳон b) feet
  c) nose    c) ear   c) mouth
Read the text and retell it. Матнро хонед ва онро
нақл кунед.
I have got a head, a trunk, arms and legs. They are all parts of 
my body. I have black hair and a round face. My nose is long. My 
neck is long, too. My shoulders are strong. My hands and legs are 
not small. My hands have 10 fingers and my feet have ten toes. I can 
see with my eyes. I can hear with my ears.

  a) 2;   b) 1;   c) 3.

  a) nose;  b) eyes;  c) ears.
3. We can smell and breathe with our ______.
  a) shoulder; b) neck;  c) nose.
4. We can eat and speak with our_______.
  a) mouth;  b) head;  c) trunk.
5. We walk with our_________.
  a) hand;   b) teeth;  c) feet.
2. Choose the correct answer. Ҷавоби дурустро интихоб 
намоед.
  a) head  a) eye  a) leg
   мӯй  b) hair гӯш  b) trunk    даҳон  b) feet
  c) nose  c) ear  c) mouth
Read the text and retell it. Матнро хонед ва онро 
нақл кунед.
I have got a head, a trunk, arms, and legs. They are 
all parts of my body. I have black hair and a round face. 
My nose is long. My neck is long, too. My shoulders are 
strong. My hands and legs are not small. My hands have

The clothes can be old or new, cheap or expensive, large or 
small. We put on hats, caps, skullcaps on our head. We put socks, 
shoes, boots on our feet. The men wear shirts and ties; the women 
wear dresses, blouses, and skirts. When it is cold we wear coats, 
snowsuits, glover, mittens, boots.

III. Learn the poem. Шеърро аз ёд кунед.
Handy, Pandy, Jack is dandy.
Loves plum cake and sugar candy,
He bought some at the grocer’s shop
And out he came, hop, hop, hop!
IV . Choose the correct word. Калимаи дурустро интихоб кунед.
1. I Like (milk, door) for breakfast.
2. You have (a cake, door) for birthday,
3. He likes to eat (chalk, biscuit) with tea.

A: Yes, you may. You can give me your cake.
B: Thank you.

Расми ягон ашёро кашида, 5 ҷумла тартиб диҳед.

[ɔ] cock, sausage, chocolate
[ʌ] brush, butter, honey
Breakfast is the first meal of our day. For breakfast Tajik people 
eat porridge, cheese, sausage, scone with butter or bread with jam. 
Many people like ice-cream and chocolate very much. They drink 
tea, milk and coffee.

III. Read the text and translate it. Матнро хонед ва тарҷума
кунед.
I see a girl. She is young. She is 
with her mother. The girl and her 
mother are at the grocery. She sees 
the sweets and an ice-cream at the 
grocery. She is asking her mother 
to buy an ice-cream for her. She is 
saying to her mother:
– Mummy, give me that cup of 
ice-cream, please.
young [jʌŋ] – ҷавон, хурд
with [wɪð] – бо ҳамроҳии
her mother [həː ˈmʌðə] – модари ӯ
sweets [swiːts] – шириниҳо
she is asking [ʃiː ɪz ˈɑːskɪŋ] – ӯ хоҳиш карда истодааст
ice-cream [ˈaɪsˈkriːm] – яхмос
cup [kʌp] – пиёла
IV . Answer the questions. Ба саволҳо ҷавоб 
гардонед.
1. What ...?
2. Is ...?
3. What does ...?
4. Is she asking ... ?
5. Who is she ... to?
V . Write these sentences. Ҷумлаҳоро нависед.

I see a girl. She is young. 
She is with her mother . The girl 
and her mother are at the gro-
cery. She sees the sweets and 
an ice-cream at the grocery. She 
is asking her mother to buy an 
ice-cream for her . She is saying 
to her mother:
— Mummy, give me that cup 
of ice-cream, please.

Мардуми Тоҷикистон намудҳои гуногу -

зиш машғул ҳастанд.
Wrestling, boxing, fighting, 
swimming, archery, throwing the hammer 
and others.
We know the famous Tajik athletes as: Azalsho Olimov 
(wrestling), Saidmumin Rahimov (wrestling), Ibrohim Hasanov 
(boating), Mikhail Lobanov and Yuri Lobanov — the brothers 
(boating), Rasul Bokiev (wrestling), Dilshod Nazarov (throwing 
the hammer), Zebunisso Rustamova (archery), Mavzuna 
Chorieva (boxing for girls) and many others. We are proud with 
our athletes.

LESSON 25. SPORTS

I. Read the words. Калимаҳоро хонед.
[ei] play, rain, game, lay, lazy, playground
[h] who, whose, whom, whole, wholesome, whole food
[ai] night, right, fight, light, fly, flight, You are right! All right!
II. Read and play during your leisure. Хонед ва дар вақти холигиатон 
иҷро кунед.
There is a good playground in front of our school. Every day 
we play different games with our classmates there. One of these 
games is “Whom do you choose from us?” It is a national children 
game. Children divide into two groups (teams). These groups of 
players stand on the two lines opposite each other at a distance of 
10–15 meters. They hold each others strongly by hands. Then after 
the casting of lots one of the groups of players begins to say:
“Here you are, take a chance!
Whom do you choose from us?” all the others repeat after him 
(her): 
The opposite players name one of the members of that group. 
That boy (or girl) runs to the line of the standing of players and 
throws himself (herself) into the hands of holders. If he (she) 
divides them, he (she) chooses one of them and takes him (her) 
with himself (herself) to his (her) line. If he (she) cannot divide

the line of them, he (she) stands with them on the opposite line. 
The winner will be that group, which they can gather more of 
players at the end of the game.

III. Read and say what a man wants to buy. Хонед ва гӯед, ки нафаре 
чиро харидан мехоҳад.
This is a shop. There are shirts, trousers, boots, shoes, socks and 
stocking in it. I see these things there. I am here with my father. My 
father wants to buy a new T-shirt for me. He is asking the shop-keeper:
– Please, show me that white T-shirt.
Shop-keeper: “Here you are. It’s very nice.”
My father: “How much does it cost?”
Shop-keeper: “Twenty somoni and fifty dirams.”
My father (puts money on the board): “Here you are.”
Shop-keeper: “Thank you for your purchase.”

wants to buy boots for me.
V. Answer the questions. Ба саволҳо 
ҷавоб гардонед.
1. What is this?
2. What do you see in the shop?
3. Who is in the shop?
4. What does he want to buy?
Write sentences with these words. Бо калимаҳои 
зерин ҷумла нависед. 
skirt, dress, jacket, sweeter .

ба саволҳо ҷавоб дода тавонанд.
I. Look and answer the questions.
Ба расм нигариста ба саволҳо ҷавоб диҳед.
THE DAY OF KNOWLEDGE
1. What is this?
2. Is it a big or a small school?
3. Whom do you see in the picture?

52
I. Read the pronouns. Ҷонишинҳоро хонед.
I  — my — me
you — your — you
he  — his — him
she — her — her
it  — its — it
II. Read the text and find pronouns. Матнро хонед ва ҷонишинҳоро 
ёбед.
This is a jacket. And that is a suit. This jacket is mine. It is very 
warm. But the suit is not mine. It is my brother’s. He likes his suit and 
I like my jacket. Last week my mother bought this jacket and that suit. 
My brother and I like our mother very much. And what about you?
jacket [ˈʤækɪt] – курткаи боронӣ
suit [sjuːt] – костюм
bought [bɔːt] – харид
last week [lɑːst wiːk] – ҳафтаи гузашта
our mother [ˈaʊə ˈmʌðə] – модарамон
and what about you? [ænd wɔt əˈbaʊt juː] – Шумо чӣ?
mine [maɪn] – аз они ман
III. Look at the picture and answer the 
questions. Ба расм нигоҳ карда, ба саволҳо ҷавоб 
гардонед.
1. Is this a jacket?
2. Is that a jacket, too?
3. Whose jacket is this?
4. Whose jacket is that?
5. Who likes his (her) jacket?

II. Read the text. Матнро хонед.
An orange is fruit. It grows on the orange-tree. Peel the orange

always sour, when we squeeze it.

тоза кардан

LESSON 36. AT THE TABLE
Competency: The pupils should read the text, answer the
questions and retell the text.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд матнро хонда, ба саволҳо
ҷавоб диҳанд ва онро накл карда тавонанд.
I. Read to the text and do the test. Матнро хонед ва тестҳоро кор 
кунед.
When the people have guests, they lay the table. First 
they spread the tablecloth and bring a big plate with bread 
or scone, jam, sugar-basin, sweets, nuts, biscuits and other 
things.
They put napkins, a teapot and some tea-cups on the 
tablecloth.
1. When the people go to see each other, they ______.
a) clean the table; b) sit on the table; c) lay the table.
2. First we _________ then we bring a big plate with bread or 
scones, jam, sweets, nuts, biscuits and other things.
a) spread the tablecloth; b) read the table; c) sit at the table.
3. They put ________ a teapot and some tea-cups on the 
tablecloth.
a) socks; b) caps; c) napkins.
II. Choose the correct answer. Ҷавоби дурустро интихоб кунед.

LESSON 38. AT SCHOOL
Competency: The pupils should read the text, answer the
questions and retell the text.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд матнро хонда, ба саволҳо
ҷавоб диҳанд ва онро нақл карда тавонанд.
I. Read. Хонед.
[aɪ] my, night, right, high
[h] have, I have, you have, she has, he has
II. Read the text and ask the questions. Матнро хонед ва оид ба он са -
волҳо тартиб диҳед.
I am at school. We are in the classroom. The teacher shows his 
book and says:
— I have a book. Here it is. Have you, Tolib, a book?
1. Where...?   3. Who shows ...?
2. Who is...?   4. Who says...?
III. Work in pairs.
Ду нафарӣ шуда кор кунед.
For example:
A: Have you a...?
B: Yes, I have.
A: Give me, please
B: All right. Take it.
A: Thank you very much.
B: Not at all.
IV . Play a game “Who wants to be a teacher?”. Бозии “Кӣ омӯзгор шудан 
мехоҳад?”-ро иҷро кунед.
V . Write. Use “have” or “has”. Нависед. “have” ва “has”- po истифода 
баред. 69

questions and retell the text.

ch [tj] сhair, chess, chalk, cheese
wr [r] wrong, write, wrist-watch
II. Read the dialogue. Муколамаро хонед.
At the lesson
Teacher: “Good morning!”
Pupils: “Good morning!”
Teacher: “Who is on duty today?”
Pupil: “I am on duty today. My name is Ravshani Pirzod.”
Teacher: “What date is it today?”
Pupil: “Today is Monday. The seventeenth of October. The day is 
warm. The pupils are all present. The classroom is clean.”
Teacher (shows a book): “Can you say, please, whose book is this? 
I have found it yesterday after lessons.”
One of the pupil: “It is my book. It’s mine.”

[ɪ] brick, chimney, chimney-pot

You can see windows, doors, and a roof, a chimney, and a 
chimney-pot, bricks in the house. It has a tile, in the kitchen and in 
the lavatory.
III. Look at the picture and answer the 
questions. Ба расм нигоҳ карда, ба саволҳо ҷа-
воб диҳед.
1. Is this a house?
2. Is this a house or a flat?
3. This is a house, isn’t it?
4. What is this?
IV . Write. What things are there in the house? Нависед. Дар хона ка -
дом ашё ҷойгир аст?

VI. Complete the sentences. Ҷумлаҳоро пурра кунед.
1. The house has a white f____ and a grey r_____.
2. It has 2 f____ and 4 w_____.
3. The house is very big. There are 2 b_____.
4. You can see 5 r_____ in it.
5. You can see s______ at the door.
V . Make up your own dialogue.
Муколамаи худатонро тартиб диҳед.
For example:
— Have you a house?
— Yes,I have. I have a house.
— What has your house?
— My house has a door, windows,
walls, floor, stairs and 4 rooms.

I. Read and learn this poem. Шеърро хонед ва аз ёд кунед.
My little house
I have a little house
With windows and a door.
One chimney on the top
And a plot of a grass before.
I have a little house
With curtains and a blind,
One chimney on the top
And a plot of a grass behind.
I have a little house
Where I go in and out.
One chimney on the top
And a garden all about.
II. Describe the picture. Расмро тавсиф 
кунед.

Расми хонаатонро кашида, дар бораи он 
нақл кунед.

кашида, онро тавсиф кунед.

LESSON 47. A SITTING – ROOM
Competency: The pupils should read the text, answer the
questions and retell the text.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд матнро хонда, ба саволҳо
ҷавоб диҳанд ва онро нақл карда тавонанд.
Words: armchair, wardrobe, bookcase, clock, mirror, living 
room.
I. Look and say.
Нигаред ва нақл кунед.
II. Read. Хонед.
[ʧ]  chalk, chicken, armchair, picture
[k]  cook, clock, bookcase.
This is our sitting-room. It is very large. You can see 2 
armchairs, a bookcase, a clock, a mirror and some pictures on the 
wall.
III. Look at the picture and answer 
the questions. Ба расм нигоҳ карда, ба 
саволҳо ҷавоб диҳед.
1. Is this a sitting room?
2. Is this a sitting room or a 
bedroom?
3. This is a sitting room, isn’t it?
4. What can you see in the sitting 
room?
V . Write down and learn the poem. Шеърро навишта, аз ёд кунед.

1. In junior school they study ... years.
 a) 4;    b) 3;     c) 2.
2. When they are 11 years old they go to... school.
 a) primary;   b) high;    c) secondary.
3. After finishing a secondary school they go to ... school.
 a) secondary;  b) comprehensive;   c) high.

 a) 6;    b) 3;     c) 4.
5. The beginning of the school year is in ...
 a) November;  b) October;    c) September.
6. The ending of the school year is in ...
 a) June;   b) May;    c) August.
7. The British students have the ... school year.
 a) shortest;   b) longest;    c) coldest.

LESSON 50. LET’S LEARN GRAMMAR
Competency: The pupils should read the text, answer the
questions and retell the text.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд матнро хонда, ба саволҳо
ҷавоб диҳанд ва онро нақл карда тавонанд.
I. Use “have” or “has”. “Have” вa “has”-po истифода кунед.
1. I _________ opened the door.
2. Nozanin _________ played with her doll.
3. Komron _________ played football.
4. We _________ read books.
5. They _________ come to school early.
II. Listen, repeat and remember. Хонед, такрор кунед ва дар хотир 
нигоҳ доред.
be — was, were — been   будан
become — became — become  шудан
buy — bought — bought   харидан
bring — brought — brought  овардан
do — did — done    кардан
give — gave — given   додан
1. I have just opened the door.
2. Grandmother has already cooked supper.
3. I have just bought a cake.84
LESSON 50. LET’S LEARN GRAMMAR
Competency: The pupils should read the text, answer the 
questions and retell the text.

ҷавоб диҳанд ва онро нақл карда тавонанд.
        I        She           We
       You   He       You
    It       They

намоед.

2. Nozanin _________ played her doll.
3. Komron _________  played football.
4. We _________ read a book.
5. They _________ come to school early.
II. Listen, repeat and remember. Хонед, такрор кунед ва 
дар хотир нигоҳ доред.

become — became — become  шудан
buy — bought — bought   харидан
bring — brought — brought   овардан
do — did — done     кардан
give — gave — given    додан

LESSON 51. MY MUMMY
Competency: The pupils should read the text, answer the
questions and retell the text.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд матнро хонда, ба саволҳо
ҷавоб диҳанд ва онро нақл карда тавонанд.
I. Learn the poem. Шеърро аз ёд кунед.
My dear, dear Mummy,
I love you very much.
I want you to be happy,
On the 8th of March.
Be happy, be happy,
On the 8th of March!
March is the first month of spring. On the 8th of March we 
celebrate our Mother's day. We give our presents to our mothers, to 
our sisters and to the girls of our class.
Let’s learn Grammar
1. I have not opened the door.
2. I have never seen this boy.
3. Dilnoza has not read the book.
4. Komron has not done his homework yet.
Negative form have past
has + not+ participle
Never, yet, ever
Have not = haven't
Has not = hasn't

инкорӣ нависед.

cup.

LESSON 53. NA VRUZ
Competency: The pupils should read the text, answer the
questions and retell the text.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд матнро хонда, ба саволҳо
ҷавоб диҳанд ва онро нақл карда тавонанд.
I. Read the text and translate it. Матнро хонед ва нақл кунед.
Navruz
On the 21st of March we celebrate 
Navruz. Navruz is our National New Year. 
Our mothers cook a lot of different meals on 
Navruz. Sumanak is the symbol of Navruz.
We play games, sing songs and wish 
Happy Navruz to our friends. After Navruz 
we have spring school holidays. It lasts till 
the 1st of April.
Last year they put one of the highest sumanak in the Dusti avenue of 
Dushanbe. It’s length was about 30 meters.90
Write 3 forms of the irregular verbs as many as you 
can.
Write the Interrogative form of the Present Perfect 
Tense. Шакли саволии феълро нависед.
l. You have been to Dushanbe.
2. I have forgotten my friend's address.
3. Nozanin has already taken her bag.
4. Dilnoza has just written the letter .
5. They have already seen the film.

Present Perfect special wh-questions
Presentation.
I. Answer the questions. Ба саволҳо ҷавоб гардонед.
What have you just opened?
I have just opened the door.
Where have you been? I have been in Dushanbe.
Question have      past
words + has + subject    +    participle
REMEMBER! WHO + HAS
For example: I have just opened the door. Who has just opened 
the door?
II. Read and learn three forms of the irregular verbs. Ce шакли 
феълҳои нодурустро хонед ва дар хотир нигоҳ доред.
grow — grew — grown   сабзидан
hear — heard — heard   шунидан
keep — kept — kept    нигоҳ доштан
know — knew — known   донистан
leave — left — left    тарк кардан
lose — lost — lost    гум кардан
run — run — ran    давидан
send — sent — sent    фиристодан
III. Read the dialogue and find Present Perfect Tense. Муколамаро 
хонед ва феъли замони ҳозираро муайян кунед.
K: “Have you heard about Anvar?”91

cook [kʊk] — пухтан, ошпаз  till [tɪl] — тo
different [ˈdɪfrənt] — гуногун  last [lɑːst] — давом мекунанд

N: “No, he has just phoned.”
K: “Really?”
N: “Sure.”

In the classroom
Komron has come to school early. He has already opened the 
windows of their classroom and he has refreshed the air. Komron 
has watered the flowers.
He has brought the chalk and has cleaned the blackboard. He 
has also dusted the teacher's table, the desks.

For example:   A: Has he come early?
        B: Yes, he has.

B: He has...
IV . Read and learn three forms of irregular verbs. Ce шакли феълҳоро 
хонед ва дар хотир нигоҳ доред.
сatch – caught – caught  қапидан
sell – sold – sold   фурӯхтан
fall – fell – fallen   афтидан
eat – ate – eaten   хӯрдан
ring – rang – rung   занг задан
sing – sang – sung   сароидан
sleep – slept – slept   хоб рафтан
speak – spoke – spoken  гaп задан
V . Play the game by your choice. Ягон бозии дилхоҳатонро иҷро 
кунед.

LESSON 55. TEST YOURSELF
Competency: The pupils should read the text, answer the
questions and retell the text.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд матнро хонда, ба саволҳо
ҷавоб диҳанд ва онро нақл карда тавонанд.
I. Choose the right answer. Ҷавоби дурустро интихоб кунед.
1. Komron _______ grown the flowers.
a) have;  b) had;  c) has.
2. Nozanin and Dilnoza _____ just played volley-ball.
a) have;  b) had;  c) has.
3. She _______ already washed the plates.
a) has;  b) have;  c) had.
4. Has he _______ lived in Dushanbe.
a) ever;  b) never;  c) no.
5. She has _______ sung a song.
a) ever;  b) never;  c) no.
6. Dilnoza _______ already _______ the text.
a) is reading; b) has read;  c) have read.
7. We _______ just _______ the soup.
a) is eating; b) was eaten; c) have eaten.94

LESSON 56. DUTY
Competency: The pupils should read the text, answer the

I. Read, the text band translate it. Матнро хонед ва тарҷума кунед.
In the classroom
Komron is a pupil. He is in the fourth form. Nozanin is a pupil, 
too. She is Komron's classmate. Komron and Nozanin are on duty 
today. They both must come to school early. They are good children 
and they are also good pupils. Nozanin cleans the teacher's table and 
the desks of pupils. Komron brings water for flowers of the class. He 
prepare those things which are necessary for the lessons. They do all 
things well.
II. Learn negative form of the modal verb of must. Шакли инкории 
феълҳои модалиро хонед.
1. This cat must sleep here.
2. You must not go out today.
3. I must not write six sentences.
4. I must write five sentences.95

a) has done;  b) have done; c) not doing.

On duty

He must prepare those things which are necessary for the 
lessons. They must do all things well.

III. Learn the interrogative form of the given sentences. Шакли саво -
лии ҷумлаҳои додашударо ёд гиред.
1. May I take my sister with me to the park, Mummy?
– Of course, my dear.

– Of course, you may.
Work in pairs. Дунафарӣ шуда кор кунед.
A: Whera do you go every day?
B: I go to... 
Read  the  dialogue  and  say  what  does  Ahmad  do.
Муколамаро   хонед   ва   гӯед,   ки   Аҳмад   чӣ   кор
мекунад.
Teacher: “Who is on duty today?”
Ahmad: “I am. My name is Ahmad.”
Teacher: “What date is it today?”
Ahmad: “Today is Monday, the 2nd of April.”
Teacher: “What is the weather like today?”
Ahmad: “It is warm today. The sun is shining brightly and the 
weather is fine.”
Teacher: “Who is absent?”
Ahmad: “Halimova Zuhro is absent.”
Teacher: “What is the matter with her? Is she ill?”
Ahmad: “No, she is not. She is in the country with her grandparents. 
Her mother says, that she will come soon.”
Teacher: “Oh, that's good. Are you ready for today's lessons?”
All the pupils: “Yes, we are.”
Teacher: “What was your home task for today's lesson?”96

II. Chain drill. Машқу тамрини дастаҷамъӣ:
A: “I play volley-ball. What about you? “
B: “I go in for swimming. And you?...”
A: “Do you play football?”
B: “Yes, I do. What about you?”

Many people like to go in for sports. I like volley-ball. Komron, 
Behruz and Jafar go in for football. Jafar and Dilshod like basketball. 
My brother goes in for riding. In summer many people go swimming.
IV . Work in pairs. Дунафарӣ шуда, кор кунед.
Say what Dilshod, Komron, Behruz, Jafar like.
For example: A: What does Behruz like?
   B: He likes football.

дом бозиро шумо ва рафиқатон дӯст медоред.
For example:  I like ________.
   He (she) likes _________.96

games, athletics, indoor games, fan,

нед.

хонагиро қайд кунед.
football    riding  hockey   swimming
volley-ball  boxing  basketball   tennis
athletics   golf   chess    draughts

There are many fans in Tajikistan. They watch all kinds of 
competitions. Outdoor games are played in the open air. Indoor 
games are played in the rooms. All my friends go in for many kinds 99

You can take a flower.

It is waiting for us.

go in for many kinds of sports, such as gymnastics, foot-

раи бозиҳои беруна ва дохили навис

Spring
March, April and May are spring months. It is a very beautiful

spring.

a) October, November, December;
b) March, April, May;
c) June, July, August.

a) school; b) park; c) town.
3.We like ___________ very much.
a) spring; b) summer; c) winter.

ёбед.

4. Офтоб  a) sky   b) sun   c) song

IV . Read and learn the poem. Шеър -
ро хонед.
Spring, spring, spring!
The trees are green,
Blue skies are seen,
Grey winter’s gone away,
The world looks new and gay.
V . Read the dialogue. Муколамаро хонед.
A: “Hello, my friend Gulnoza!”
B: “Hello, Dilnoza. What season do you like?”
A: “Oh, my dear friend, I like spring. Do you like spring?”
B: “Oh, no. I like summer very much.”
A: “Why?”
B: “Because we have got school holidays in summer.”
Make up your own dialogue. Муколамаи  худро  тар-
тиб диҳед.

LESSON 67. A BATHROOM
Competency: The pupils should read the text, answer the 
questions and retell the text.
Салоҳият: Хонандагон бояд матнро хонда, ба саволҳо 
ҷавоб диҳанд ва онро нақл карда тавонанд.

119
Summer, summer, summer!
The sun is shining
The flowers are blooming,
The sky is blue,
The rains are few.
II. Read the text and answer the 
questions. Матнро хонед ва ба саволҳо ҷа -
воб диҳед.
Fresh water
Summer comes after spring. June, July and August are summer 
months. The weather is very hot in summer. We don’t go to school. 
We have summer holidays. We help our parents in the fields and 
gardens. And usually we go to the river for swimming. The water 
in the river is very cold. It is fresh and clean. So, our friends and I 
like to go to swim in summer.
The President of our republic Emomali Rakhmon declared the 
2003 th-year the year of fresh water. We must not pollute water!
1) What season is it?
2) What are summer months?
3) What is the weather like in summer?
4) Who helps the parents?
5) Who goes to swim?
6) Do you like summer?
III. Find the missing words and write down. Калимаҳои партофта -
шударо нависед.

Read the text. Матнро хонед.
Autumn
The days become shorter in autumn than in summer. The nights 
become longer and cooler. In Tajikistan the people pick cotton in 
autumn. They gather the harvest in the fields and fruit in the gardens.
In the morning it is cool. But in the daytime and evenings the 
weather becomes warmer than usual. I like the nature of Tajikistan.
